# 07 — Source atlas

> **Reference page:** Beginners can skip this file. It is a searchable inventory for locating source, tests, configuration, workflows, and prototypes—not an explanation to read from top to bottom.

This generated atlas accounts for every code-like file currently checked into the repository, excluding dependency, build, target, and coverage directories. It contains **3389 files** totaling **45187208 bytes**.

> Generated from the worktree file list. A row means the file was accounted for; use the linked source and the explanatory chapters for behavioral interpretation. Regenerate after source-tree changes.

## Area key

- `src`: recovered CLI/runtime core plus the expanded local server.
- `desktop`: Electron host, React renderer, sidecars, native resources, and desktop tests.
- `adapters`: messaging-platform bridges.
- `runtime`: Python Computer Use helpers.
- `scripts` / `.github`: quality, maintenance, release, and CI code.
- `site` / `docs`: documentation application and interactive prototypes.
- `tests` / `fixtures`: cross-area verification and test data.
- `root` / other: package configuration, preload, stubs, and launch support.
